String(prod.id)])));\r\n        setInspectedProduct(null);\r\n        setAlreadyOwnedProduct(prod);\r\n        return;\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      const listedPrice = Number(prod.listed_price_dollars || 0);\r\n\r\n      if (listedPrice <= 0) {\r\n        if (freshProf) {\r\n          await grantStoreProductToUser(prod, activeUserId, freshProf, false);\r\n          setInspectedProduct(null);\r\n        }\r\n        return;\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      const returnUrl = `${window.location.origin}/?view=shop&store_product_success=${encodeURIComponent(\r\n        prod.id\r\n      )}`;\r\n\r\n      const prod.product_type === 'bundle' && commCreators.length > 0\r\n          ?     "src/MainViews/MyFaves.tsx": "import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';\r\nimport { Search, ChevronRight, ArrowLeft, Play, Library, ChevronLeft } from 'lucide-react';\r\nimport { CLOUDFLARE_BASE_URL = 'https://pub-180171f859f64aa7aadb7001a6b96e65.r2.dev';\r\n\r\nconst getPatternStyle = (color: string, pattern: string) => {\r\n  const baseColor = color || '#18181b';\r\n  const overlay = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.2)';\r\n  if (pattern === 'dots') return { backgroundColor: baseColor, backgroundImage: `radial-gradient(${overlay} 2px, transparent 2px)`, backgroundSize: '12px 12px' };\r\n  if (pattern === 'lines') return { backgroundColor: baseColor, backgroundImage: `repeating-linear-gradient(45deg, transparent, transparent 5px, ${overlay} 5px, ${overlay} 10px)` };\r\n  if (pattern === 'grid') return { backgroundColor: baseColor, backgroundImage: `linear-gradient(${overlay} 1px, transparent 1px), linear-gradient(90deg, ${overlay} 1px, transparent 1px)`, backgroundSize: '20px 20px' };\r\n  if (pattern === 'pinstripes') return { backgroundColor: baseColor, backgroundImage: `repeating-linear-gradient(45deg, ${overlay} 0, ${overlay} 1px, transparent 1px, transparent 8px)` };\r\n  if (pattern === 'mesh') return { backgroundColor: baseColor, backgroundImage: `linear-gradient(${overlay} 1px, transparent 1px), linear-gradient(90deg, ${overlay} 1px, transparent 1px)`, backgroundSize: '14px 14px' };\r\n  if (pattern === 'glow') return { backgroundColor: baseColor, backgroundImage: `radial-gradient(circle at 50% 0%, rgba(255,255,255,0.15) 0%, transparent 70%)` };\r\n  if (pattern === 'cut') return { backgroundColor: baseColor, backgroundImage: `linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(255,255,255,0.04) 50%, transparent 50%)` };\r\n  return { backgroundColor: baseColor };\r\n};\r\n\r\nexport const Favorites = ({ userTier = 'visitor', setActiveTab, onNavigate }: any) => {\r\n  const { seriesList = [], isLoading } = useSeriesData();\r\n  const [myFaves, setMyFaves] = useState(0);\r\n  const [touchStartX, setTouchStartX] = useState(0);\r\n  const [touchEndX, setTouchEndX] = useState(0);\r\n\r\n  const isPlusSubscriber = userTier === 'premium';\r\n  const suggestedSeries = seriesList.filter((s: any) => !s.is_hidden).slice(0, 5);\r\n\r\n  const fetchUserData = useCallback(async () => {\r\n    const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();\r\n    if (!user) return;\r\n    setCurrentUser(user);\r\n\r\n    const [{ data: profile }, { data: history }] = await Promise.all([\r\n      supabase.from('profiles').select('favorites, is_premium').eq('id', user.id).maybeSingle(),\r\n      supabase.from('reading_history').select('*').eq('user_id', user.id).order('updated_at', { ascending: false })\r\n    ]);\r\n\r\n    const isPlus = isPlusSubscriber || Boolean(profile?.is_premium);\r\n\r\n    if (profile?.favorites && Array.isArray(profile.favorites) && seriesList.length > 0) {\r\n      const { data: chapterData } = await supabase\r\n        .from('chapters')\r\n        .select('series_slug, id, publish_at, is_published')\r\n        .eq('is_published', true);\r\n\r\n      const faves = seriesList\r\n        .filter((s: that have actually released for this user tier!\r\n          const releasedChapters = (chapterData || []).filter(\r\n            (c: any) =>\r\n              c.series_slug === s.slug &&\r\n              c.is_published &&\r\n              (isFreeOsw || isChapterReleasedForUser(c.publish_at, isPlus))\r\n          );\r\n          const readChaptersForSeries = (history || []).filter((h: any) =>\r\n            releasedChapters.some((sc: any) => String(sc.id) === String(h.chapter_id))\r\n          );\r\n\r\n          const clearedCount = parseInt(localStorage.getItem(`cleared_updates_${s.slug}`) || '0', 10);\r\n          const hasUpdate =\r\n            releasedChapters.length > readChaptersForSeries.length &&\r\n            releasedChapters.length > clearedCount;\r\n\r\n          return {\r\n            ...s,\r\n            has_update: hasUpdate,\r\n            current_chapter_count: releasedChapters.length\r\n          };\r\n        });\r\n\r\n      setMyFaves(faves);\r\n chap.series_slug && !s.is_hidden);\r\n            if (!series || seenSeriesSlugs.has(series.slug)) return null;\r\n            seenSeriesSlugs.add(series.slug);\r\n\r\n            return {\r\n };\r\n          })\r\n          .filter(Boolean);\r\n\r\n        setRecentReads(combined);\r\n      }\r\n    }\r\n  }, [seriesList, isPlusSubscriber]);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    fetchUserData();\r\n    window.addEventListener('seriesUpdated', fetchUserData);\r\n    window.addEventListener('vaultUpdated', fetchUserData);\r\n    window.addEventListener('progressUpdated', : fave))\r\n      );\r\n    }\r\n    if (onNavigate) onNavigate(s);\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const handleTouchStart = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {\r\n    setTouchStartX(e.targetTouches[0].clientX);\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const handleTouchMove = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {\r\n    setTouchEndX(e.targetTouches[0].clientX);\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const handleTouchEnd = () => {\r\n    if (!touchStartX || !touchEndX) activeIndex > 0) {\r\n      setActiveIndex(prev => prev - 1);\r\n    }\r\n    setTouchStartX(0);\r\n    setTouchEndX(0);\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  if (isLoading) {\r\n    return (\r\n      <div }\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-transparent text-white pb-32 relative overflow-x-hidden\">\r\n      <div className=\"sticky top-0 z-40 bg-black/90 backdrop-blur-xl px-4  </button>\r\n        <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-black italic uppercase tracking-tighter text-white pr-16\">\r\n          My Bookshelf\r\n        </h1>\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      <div     "src/MainViews/AMNewsPage.tsx": "import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';\r\nimport { ExternalLink, BookOpen, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';\r\nimport { supabase } from '../supabase';\r\n\r\nexport const AMNewsPage = ({ onBack }: any) => {\r\n  const [posts, setPosts] = useState<any[]>([]);\r\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    const awardNewsPoints = async () => {\r\n      try {\r\n        const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();\r\n        if (!user) return;\r\n\r\n        const today = new Date().toDateString();\r\n        const storageKey = `am_news_check_${user.id}`;\r\n        if (localStorage.getItem(storageKey) === today) return;\r\n\r\n        const { data: profile } = await supabase\r\n          .from('profiles')\r\n          .select('news_checks, fandom_score')\r\n          .eq('id', user.id)\r\n          .maybeSingle();\r\n\r\n        if (profile) {\r\n          await supabase\r\n            .from('profiles')\r\n            .update({\r\n              news_checks: (profile.news_checks || 0) + 1,\r\n              fandom_score: (profile.fandom_score || 0) + 2\r\n            })\r\n            .eq('id', user.id);\r\n\r\n          localStorage.setItem(storageKey, today);\r\n          window.dispatchEvent(new Event('profileUpdated'));\r\n        }\r\n      } catch (err) {\r\n        console.error('Silent error awarding news points:', err);\r\n      }\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    awardNewsPoints();\r\n  }, []);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    window.scrollTo(0, 0);\r\n    const fetchPosts = async () => {\r\n      try {\r\n        const res = await fetch('https://www.saturday-am.com/wp-json/wp/v2/posts?per_page=12&_embed');\r\n        if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to fetch blog feed');\r\n        const data = await res.json();\r\n        setPosts(Array.isArray(data) ? Transmissions...\r\n            </span>\r\n          </div>\r\n        ) : (\r\n          <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6\">\r\n            {posts.map(post => src={imageUrl}\r\n                    alt=\"Blog Thumbnail\"\r\n                    loading=\"lazy\"\r\n                    className=\"absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover     "src/MainViews/Browse.tsx": "import { useState, useMemo, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';\r\nimport { useSeriesData } from '../userSeriesData';\r\nimport { supabase } from '../supabase';\r\nimport { Search, Library, ArrowUpDown, Clock, Zap } from 'lucide-react';\r\nimport {\r\n  isCompletedRunSeries,\r\n  isActiveOneShotWednesday,\r\n  isInOneShotDebutWindow,\r\n  getOneShotFreeCountdown\r\n} from '../oneshotWednesday';\r\n\r\nconst CLOUDFLARE_BASE_URL = 'https://pub-180171f859f64aa7aadb7001a6b96e65.r2.dev';\r\n\r\nconst getPatternStyle = (color: string, pattern: string) => {\r\n  const baseColor = color || '#18181b';\r\n  const overlay = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.2)';\r\n  if (pattern === 'dots') return { backgroundColor: baseColor, backgroundImage: `radial-gradient(${overlay} 2px, transparent 2px)`, backgroundSize: '12px 12px' };\r\n  if (pattern === 'lines') return { backgroundColor: baseColor, backgroundImage: `repeating-linear-gradient(45deg, transparent, transparent 5px, ${overlay} 5px, ${overlay} 10px)` };\r\n  if (pattern === 'grid') return { backgroundColor: baseColor, backgroundImage: `linear-gradient(${overlay} 1px, transparent 1px), linear-gradient(90deg, ${overlay} 1px, transparent 1px)`, backgroundSize: '20px 20px' };\r\n  if (pattern === 'pinstripes') return { backgroundColor: baseColor, backgroundImage: `repeating-linear-gradient(45deg, ${overlay} 0, ${overlay} 1px, transparent 1px, transparent 8px)` };\r\n  if (pattern === 'mesh') return { backgroundColor: baseColor, backgroundImage: `linear-gradient(${overlay} 1px, transparent 1px), linear-gradient(90deg, ${overlay} 1px, transparent 1px)`, backgroundSize: '14px 14px' };\r\n  if (pattern === 'glow') return { backgroundColor: baseColor, backgroundImage: `radial-gradient(circle at 50% 0%, rgba(255,255,255,0.15) 0%, transparent 70%)` };\r\n  if (pattern === 'cut') return { backgroundColor: baseColor, backgroundImage: `linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(255,255,255,0.04) 50%, transparent 50%)` };\r\n  return { backgroundColor: baseColor };\r\n};\r\n\r\nconst isReleasedYet = (publishAt?: string | null) => {\r\n  if (!publishAt) return true;\r\n  return new Date(publishAt).getTime() <= Date.now();\r\n};\r\n\r\n// Synced 72-hour (3-day) NEW badge window (ignores updated_at so admin edits don't re-badge old series)\r\nconst isNewSeriesRelease = (publishAt?: string | null, createdAt?: string | null) => {\r\n  const now = Date.now();\r\n  const threeDaysMs = 3 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\r\n  const candidates = [publishAt, createdAt].filter(Boolean);\r\n  for (const dateStr of candidates) {\r\n    const t = new Date(dateStr as string).getTime();\r\n    if (!isNaN(t) && t <= now && now - t < threeDaysMs) {\r\n      return true;\r\n    }\r\n  }\r\n  return false;\r\n};\r\n\r\nexport const Browse = ({ onNavigate, userTier = 'visitor' }: any) => {\r\n  const { seriesList = [] } = useSeriesData();\r\n  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');\r\n  const [sortBy, setSortBy] = useState<'title-asc' | 'title-desc' | 'creator-asc'>('title-asc');\r\n  const [creatorsMap, setCreatorsMap] = useState<Record<string, string[]>>({});\r\n\r\n  const isPlusSubscriber = userTier === 'premium';\r\n\r\n  const fetchAllCredits = useCallback(async () => {\r\n    try {\r\n      const { data } = await supabase\r\n        .from('series_creators')\r\n        .select('series_slug, name');\r\n\r\n      if (data) {\r\n        const map: Record<string, string[]> = {};\r\n        data.forEach((row: any) => {\r\n          if (!row.series_slug || !row.name) return;\r\n          if (!map[row.series_slug]) map[row.series_slug] = [];\r\n          map[row.series_slug].push(row.name.toLowerCase());\r\n        });\r\n        setCreatorsMap(map);\r\n      }\r\n    } catch (err) {\r\n      console.error('Failed to load creator credits:', err);\r\n    }\r\n  }, []);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    fetchAllCredits();\r\n    window.addEventListener('seriesUpdated', fetchAllCredits);\r\n    window.addEventListener('vaultUpdated', fetchAllCredits);\r\n    return () => {\r\n      window.removeEventListener('seriesUpdated', fetchAllCredits);\r\n      window.removeEventListener('vaultUpdated', fetchAllCredits);\r\n    };\r\n  }, [fetchAllCredits]);\r\n\r\n  const sortedSeries = useMemo(() => {\r\n    const query = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();\r\n\r\n    const filtered = seriesList.filter((s: any) => {\r\n      if (s.is_hidden) return false;\r\n      const isFreeOswOrDebut = isActiveOneShotWednesday(s, seriesList);\r\n      if (!isFreeOswOrDebut && !isReleasedYet(s.publish_at)) return false;\r\n\r\n      if (!query) return true;\r\n\r\n      const titleMatch = s.title?.toLowerCase().includes(query);\r\n      const primaryCreatorMatch = s.creator_name?.toLowerCase().includes(query);\r\n      const coCreators = creatorsMap[s.slug] || [];\r\n      const coCreatorMatch = coCreators.some(name => name.includes(query));\r\n\r\n      return titleMatch || primaryCreatorMatch || coCreatorMatch;\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    return [...filtered].sort((a, b) => {\r\n      if (sortBy === 'title-asc') {\r\n        return (a.title || '').localeCompare(b.title || '');\r\n      }\r\n      if (sortBy === 'title-desc') {\r\n        return (b.title || '').localeCompare(a.title || '');\r\n      }\r\n      if (sortBy === 'creator-asc') {\r\n        const creatorA = (a.creator_name || '').trim().toLowerCase();\r\n        const creatorB = (b.creator_name || '').trim().toLowerCase();\r\n        if (!creatorA) return 1;\r\n        if (!creatorB) return -1;\r\n        return creatorA.localeCompare(creatorB);\r\n      }\r\n      return 0;\r\n    });\r\n  }, [seriesList, searchQuery, creatorsMap, sortBy]);\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-transparent text-white pb-24 relative px-4 pt-6\">\r\n      <div className=\"sticky top-0 z-40 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-xl pb-4 pt-2 mb-4 border-b border-zinc-800 rounded-b-2xl px-2\">\r\n        <div className=\"flex justify-between items-end mb-4 pr-16 sm:pr-20\">\r\n          <h1 className=\"text-3xl font-black italic uppercase tracking-tighter text-white drop-shadow-md\">\r\n            The Vault\r\n          </h1>\r\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-zinc-400 bg-zinc-900/80 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full border border-zinc-800\">\r\n            <Library className=\"w-4 h-4\" />\r\n            <span className=\"text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest\">\r\n              {sortedSeries.length} Series\r\n            </span>\r\n          </div>\r\n        </div>\r\n\r\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center gap-3 w-full\">\r\n          <div className=\"relative flex-1\">\r\n            <Search className=\"absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 text-zinc-500\" />\r\n            <input\r\n              type=\"text\"\r\n              placeholder=\"Search by series title or creator...\"\r\n              value={searchQuery}\r\n              onChange={e => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}\r\n              className=\"w-full bg-zinc-900/90 border border-zinc-800 text-white pl-10 pr-4 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm focus:outline-none focus:border-[#fe9a00] transition-colors shadow-inner\"\r\n            />\r\n          </div>\r\n\r\n          <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 bg-zinc-900/90 border border-zinc-800 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl\">\r\n            <ArrowUpDown className=\"w-4 h-4 text-[#fe9a00] shrink-0\" />\r\n            <select\r\n              value={sortBy}\r\n              onChange={e => setSortBy(e.target.value as any)}\r\n              aria-label=\"Sort series\"\r\n              className=\"bg-transparent text-white text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider py-1.5 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer\"\r\n            >\r\n              <option value=\"title-asc\" className=\"bg-zinc-950 text-white\">Title: A-Z</option>\r\n              <option value=\"title-desc\" className=\"bg-zinc-950 text-white\">Title: Z-A</option>\r\n              <option value=\"creator-asc\" className=\"bg-zinc-950 text-white\">Creator: A-Z</option>\r\n            </select>\r\n          </div>\r\n        </div>\r\n      </div>\r\n\r\n      {sortedSeries.length === 0 ? (\r\n        <div className=\"text-center text-zinc-500 py-16 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-xs border-2 border-dashed border-zinc-800 rounded-xl mt-8 bg-black/40\">\r\n          No series found matching \"{searchQuery}\".\r\n        </div>\r\n      ) : (\r\n        <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-4 gap-y-6 mt-6 pb-8\">\r\n          {sortedSeries.map((item: any) => {\r\n            const isCompleted = isCompletedRunSeries(item, seriesList);\r\n            const is48hDebut = isCompleted && isInOneShotDebutWindow(item, seriesList);\r\n            const showFreeWednesdayCue = !isPlusSubscriber && isCompleted && isActiveOneShotWednesday(item, seriesList);\r\n            const showPlusVaultTag = !isPlusSubscriber && isCompleted && !showFreeWednesdayCue;\r\n            const freeCountdown = showFreeWednesdayCue ? getOneShotFreeCountdown(item, seriesList) : null;\r\n            const showNewBadge = isNewSeriesRelease(item.publish_at, item.created_at);\r\n\r\n            return (\r\n              <div\r\n                key={item.id || item.slug}\r\n                className=\"flex-shrink-0 cursor-pointer group/card\"\r\n                onClick={() => onNavigate(item)}\r\n              >\r\n                <div\r\n                  className=\"relative overflow-hidden rounded-lg aspect-[2/3] border border-white shadow-[5px_5px_0px_0px_#fe9a00] group-hover/card:shadow-[8px_8px_0px_0px_#fe9a00] group-hover/card:-translate-y-1 group-hover/card:-translate-x-1 transition-all duration-300 mb-3\"\r\n                  style={getPatternStyle(item.card_color, item.card_pattern)}\r\n                >\r\n                  <div className=\"absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-transparent to-black/60 z-0\" />\r\n                  <img\r\n                    src={item.character_url || item.cover_url}\r\n                    alt={`${item.title} Character`}\r\n                    loading=\"lazy\"\r\n                    decoding=\"async\"\r\n                    className={`absolute left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 max-w-none object-contain transform transition-transform duration-500 ease-out group-hover/card:scale-[1.15] z-10 ${\r\n                      item.character_align === 'top' ? cleanCode }\r\n      );\r\n\r\n      if (!statusError && (codeStatus === 'VALID' || codeStatus === 'UNUSED' || codeStatus === true)) {\r\n        isValidCode = true;\r\n        matchedSource = 'rpc_status';\r\n      } else if (!statusError && codeStatus === 'USED') {\r\n        isAlreadyUsed = true;\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      if (!isValidCode && !isAlreadyUsed) {\r\n        const { data:       } else {\r\n              isValidCode = true;\r\n              matchedSource = 'ledger';\r\n            }\r\n          }\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      if (!isValidCode && !isAlreadyUsed) isDepleted) {\r\n            isAlreadyUsed = true;\r\n          } else {\r\n            isValidCode = true;\r\n            matchedSource = 'promo_table';\r\n            promoRowData = promoRow;\r\n          }\r\n        }\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      if (isAlreadyUsed) {\r\n        setAlertConfig({\r\n          type: 'error',\r\n          title: 'Code Used',\r\n          message: 'This premium code has r.bundle_config?.gift_month_key ||\r\n          (r.created_at\r\n            ? `${new Date(r.created_at).getFullYear()}-${String(\r\n                new Date(r.created_at).getMonth() + 1\r\n     "src/MainViews/SeriesSection.tsx": "import React, { useRef, useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';\r\nimport { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Heart, Clock, Zap } from 'lucide-react';\r\nimport { getOneShotFreeCountdown,\r\n  isChapterReleasedForUser,\r\n  PLUS_EARLY_ACCESS_MS\r\n} from '../oneshotWednesday';\r\n\r\nconst CLOUDFLARE_BASE_URL = 'https://pub-180171f859f64aa7aadb7001a6b96e65.r2.dev';\r\n\r\nconst getPatternStyle = (color: string, pattern: string) => {\r\n  const baseColor = color || '#18181b';\r\n  const overlay = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.2)'; \r\n  if (pattern === 'dots') return { backgroundColor: baseColor, backgroundImage: `radial-gradient(${overlay} 2px, transparent 2px)`, backgroundSize: '12px 12px' };\r\n  if (pattern === 'lines') return { backgroundColor: baseColor, backgroundImage: `repeating-linear-gradient(45deg, transparent, transparent 5px, ${overlay} 5px, ${overlay} 10px)` };\r\n  if (pattern === 'grid') return { backgroundColor: baseColor, backgroundImage: `linear-gradient(${overlay} 1px, transparent 1px), linear-gradient(90deg, ${overlay} 1px, transparent 1px)`, backgroundSize: '20px 20px' };\r\n  return { backgroundColor: baseColor }; \r\n};\r\n\r\nconst isNewItem = (createdAt: string | undefined) => {\r\n  if (!createdAt) return false;\r\n  return Date.now() - new Date(createdAt).getTime() < 3 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\r\n};\r\n\r\nconst formatDurationShort = (diffMs: number): string => {\r\n  const totalMinutes = Math.max(1, Math.floor(diffMs / (1000 * 60)));\r\n  const days = Math.floor(totalMinutes / (60 * 24));\r\n  const hours = Math.floor((totalMinutes % (60 * 24)) / 60);\r\n  const mins = totalMinutes % 60;\r\n\r\n  if (days > 0) return `${days}D ${hours}H`;\r\n  if (hours > 0) return `${hours}H ${mins}M`;\r\n  return `${mins}M`;\r\n};\r\n\r\n// Role-aware badge builder: PLUS users only see when their next chapter is available; Free/Visitors Date.now();\r\n  const plusUnlockMs = officialReleaseMs - PLUS_EARLY_ACCESS_MS;\r\n\r\n  if (isPlusSubscriber) {\r\n    // For PLUS accounts: if the chapter is already unlocked in Early Access, don't diffForPlus <= 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\r\n    return {\r\n      badgeText: `NEXT CH IN ${timeStr}`,\r\n      shortText: `CH IN ${timeStr}`,\r\n      isSoon\r\n    };\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  // For Free accounts & Visitors: show Early Access badge before/during the PLUS window, and standard countdown to public release\r\n  const diffForFree = officialReleaseMs - now;\r\n  if (diffForFree <= 0) return null;\r\n\r\n  const diffToPlusUnlock = plusUnlockMs - now;\r\n  const freeTimeStr = formatDurationShort(diffForFree);\r\n\r\n  if (diffToPlusUnlock > 0 && diffToPlusUnlock <= 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) {\r\n    const plusTimeStr = formatDurationShort(diffToPlusUnlock);\r\n    return {\r\n      badgeText: `PLUS DROP IN ${plusTimeStr}`,\r\n      shortText: `CH IN ${freeTimeStr}`,\r\n      isSoon: true\r\n    };\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  if (diffToPlusUnlock <= 0 && diffForFree > 0) {\r\n    return {\r\n      badgeText: `EARLY ACCESS LIVE • FREE IN ${freeTimeStr}`,\r\n      shortText: `FREE IN ${freeTimeStr}`,\r\n      isSoon: true\r\n    };\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  return {\r\n    badgeText: `NEXT CH IN ${freeTimeStr}`,\r\n    shortText: `CH IN ${freeTimeStr}`,\r\n    isSoon: diffForFree <= 24 * 60 .from('chapters')\r\n        .select('series_slug, publish_at, chapter_number, is_published')\r\n        .in('series_slug', slugs)\r\n        .eq('is_published', true)\r\n        .gt('publish_at', const aIsDebut = isInOneShotDebutWindow(a, series);\r\n      const bIsDebut = isInOneShotDebutWindow(b, series);\r\n      if (aIsDebut && !bIsDebut) return -1;\r\n      if (!aIsDebut && bIsDebut) -1;\r\n      if (!aIsSpotlight && bIsSpotlight) return 1;\r\n      return 0;\r\n    });\r\n  }, [series, isPlusSubscriber, title, hasAnyActiveFreeCompletedRun]);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    if \r\n    const fetchLikes = async () => {\r\n      const slugs = seriesSlugs.split(',').filter(Boolean);\r\n      if (slugs.length === 0) return;\r\n\r\n      const { data, error } = await boolean> = {};\r\n        data.forEach((row: any) => { map[row.series_slug] = true; });\r\n        setLocalLikes(map);\r\n      }\r\n    };\r\n    fetchLikes();\r\n  }, [currentUser?.id, seriesSlugs]);\r\n\r\n  const scroll = (direction: 'left' | 'right') => {\r\n    if (scrollRef.current) {\r\n      const container = scrollRef.current;\r\n      const step = Math.max(240, const handleToggleLike = (e: React.MouseEvent, seriesSlug: string) => {\r\n    e.preventDefault();\r\n    e.stopPropagation();\r\n    \r\n    if (!currentUser?.id) {\r\n      if (onRequireAuth) user_id: currentUser.id, series_slug: seriesSlug });\r\n        } else {\r\n          await supabase.from('series_likes').insert([{ user_id: currentUser.id, series_slug: seriesSlug }]);\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        const { data } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('fandom_score').eq('id', currentUser.id).maybeSingle();\r\n        if (data) {\r\n          const newScore = newLikedState \r\n            ? (data.fandom_score || 0) + 1 \r\n            : Math.max(0, (data.fandom_score || 0) - 1);\r\n\r\n          await supabase.from('profiles').update({ fandom_score: newScore }).eq('id', currentUser.id);\r\n          window.dispatchEvent(new Event('profileUpdated'));\r\n        }\r\n      } catch (err: any) {\r\n        console.error(\"Silently caught like error:\", err.message);\r\n      }\r\n    };\r\n\r\n    syncWithDatabase();\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  if (!series || series.length === 0) return null;\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className=\"mb-10 relative group\">\r\n 150);\r\n    }\r\n  }, [loading, creators]);\r\n\r\n  const processedCreators = useMemo(() => {\r\n    const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();\r\n    const result = creators.filter(c =>\r\n setIsLoading(true);\r\n    try {\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n        .from('series_characters')\r\n        .select('*')\r\n        .or('is_hidden.is.null,is_hidden.eq.false')\r\n        .order('id', { ascending: true });\r\n      if (error) throw error;\r\n      const updatedRoles = (data || []).map(c => ({\r\n        ...c,\r\n        role_type: c.role_type === 'Hero' ? 'Support' : c.role_type\r\n      }));\r\n      setRawCharacters(updatedRoles);\r\n    } catch (err) {\r\n      console.error('Failed to fetch characters:', err);\r\n      setRawCharacters([]);\r\n    } finally {\r\n      setIsLoading(false);\r\n    }\r\n  }, []);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    fetchCharacters();\r\n    window.addEventListener('seriesUpdated', fetchCharacters);\r\n    window.addEventListener('vaultUpdated', fetchCharacters);\r\n    return () => {\r\n      window.removeEventListener('seriesUpdated', fetchCharacters);\r\n      window.removeEventListener('vaultUpdated', fetchCharacters);\r\n    };\r\n  }, [fetchCharacters]);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!currentUser || !rawCharacters.length) return;\r\n    const .select('character_id')\r\n        .eq('user_id', currentUser.id)\r\n        .in('character_id', charIds);\r\n      if (data) {\r\n        const likesMap: Record<string, boolean> = {};\r\n        data.forEach(row => {\r\n          likesMap[row.character_id] = true;\r\n        });\r\n        setCharLikes(likesMap);\r\n      }\r\n    };\r\n    fetchCharLikes();\r\n  }, [currentUser, rawCharacters]);\r\n\r\n  const characters = useMemo(() => {\r\n    if (!Array.isArray(rawCharacters)) return [];\r\n    const safeSeriesList = Array.isArray(seriesList) ? seriesList : [];\r\n    return rawCharacters.map(c => {\r\n      const s = safeSeriesList.find((series: any) => series?.slug === c?.series_slug);\r\n      return { ...c, series_title: s?.title || 'Unknown Series' };\r\n    });\r\n  }, [rawCharacters, seriesList]);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (isLoading || characters.length === 0) return;\r\n    const targetId = sessionStorage.getItem('am_target_character_id');\r\n    const targetName = sessionStorage.getItem('am_target_character_name');\r\n    if (!targetId && !targetName) return;\r\n\r\n    sessionStorage.removeItem('am_target_character_id');\r\n    sessionStorage.removeItem('am_target_character_name');\r\n\r\n    const matched = characters.find(\r\n      (c: any) =>\r\n        (targetId && String(c.id) === String(targetId)) ||\r\n        (targetName &&\r\n          String(c.name || '')\r\n            .trim()\r\n            .toLowerCase() === String(targetName).trim().toLowerCase())\r\n    );\r\n    if (matched) {\r\n      setSelectedChar(matched);\r\n      setActiveTab('combat');\r\n    }\r\n  }, [isLoading, characters]);\r\n\r\n  const uniqueAffiliations = Array.from(new Set((characters || []).map(c => c?.element).filter(Boolean))).sort();\r\n\r\n  const groupedChars = useMemo(() => {\r\n    let result = characters || [];\r\n\r\n    if (searchQuery) {\r\n      const lowerQuery = searchQuery.toLowerCase();\r\n      result = result.filter(\r\n        c =>\r\n          (c?.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(lowerQuery) ||\r\n          (c?.series_title || '').toLowerCase().includes(lowerQuery)\r\n      );\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    const isRoleFilter = viewSelection.startsWith('RoleFilter:');\r\n    const isAffilFilter = '');\r\n      result = result.filter(c => c?.role_type === activeRole);\r\n    }\r\n    if (isAffilFilter) {\r\n      const activeAffil = viewSelection.replace('AffilFilter:', '');\r\n      result = result.filter(c => c?.element === activeAffil);\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    const groups: { name: string | null; items: any[] }[] = [];\r\n    const currentSortBy = isSort ? viewSelection.replace('Sort:', '') : 'A-Z';\r\n\r\n    if (currentSortBy === 'Role') {\r\n      const mcHeroes = result.filter(c => c?.is_mc && c?.role_type === 'Support');\r\n      const heroes = result.filter(c => !c?.is_mc && c?.role_type === 'Support');\r\n      const villains = result.filter(c => c?.role_type === 'Villain');\r\n      const neutrals = result.filter(c => c?.role_type === 'Neutral');\r\n\r\n      if (mcHeroes.length) groups.push({ name: 'Main Characters', items: mcHeroes });\r\n      if (heroes.length) groups.push({ name: 'Support', items: heroes });\r\n      if (villains.length) groups.push({ name: 'Villains', items: villains });\r\n      if (neutrals.length) groups.push({ name: 'Neutral', items: neutrals });\r\n    } else if (currentSortBy === 'Series' || currentSortBy === 'Affiliation') {\r\n      const key = currentSortBy === 'Series' ? 'series_title' : 'element';\r\n      const uniqueKeys = Array.from(new Set(result.map(c => c[key]).filter(Boolean))).sort();\r\n      uniqueKeys.forEach(k => {\r\n        groups.push({ name: k as string, items: result.filter(c => c[key] === k) });\r\n      });\r\n      const unassigned = result.filter(c => !c[key]);\r\n      if (unassigned.length) groups.push({ name: 'Unassigned', items: unassigned });\r\n    } else {\r\n      const sorted = [...result].sort((a, b) => {\r\n        if (currentSortBy === 'A-Z') return (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || '');\r\n        if (currentSortBy === 'Z-A') return (b.name || '').localeCompare(a.name || '');\r\n        return 0;\r\n      });\r\n      groups.push({ name: null, items: sorted });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    return groups;\r\n  }, [characters, searchQuery, viewSelection]);\r\n\r\n  const handleToggleGridLike = async (e: React.MouseEvent, char: any) => {\r\n    e.stopPropagation();\r\n    if (!currentUser) {\r\n      setUpsellConfig({ type: 'visitor', message: 'Create a Free Account to like characters!' });\r\n      return;\r\n    }\r\n    const currentlyLiked = charLikes[char.id];\r\n    setCharLikes(prev => ({ ...prev, [char.id]: !currentlyLiked }));\r\n\r\n    try {\r\n      if (currentlyLiked) {\r\n        await supabase.from('character_likes').delete().match({ user_id: currentUser.id, character_id: console.error('Failed to sync character like:', err);\r\n      setCharLikes(prev => ({ ...prev, [char.id]: currentlyLiked }));\r\n    }\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const openCharacterDossier = (char: any) => {\r\n    setSelectedChar(char);\r\n    setActiveTab('combat');\r\n  };\r\n\r\n  const powerChunks = useMemo(() => formatLoreChunks(selectedChar?.powers), [selectedChar?.powers]);\r\n  const [selectedChar?.origin]);\r\n\r\n  return (\r\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-transparent text-white relative z-[100] pb-48\">\r\n      {upsellConfig && (\r\n        <div\r\n          className=\"fixed inset-0 z-[7000] bg-black/90 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center p-6 animate-fade-in\"\r\n          onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}\r\n        >\r\n          <div className=\"bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 p-8 rounded-2xl w-full max-w-sm flex flex-col items-center text-center shadow-2xl relative\">\r\n            <button\r\n              onClick={() => setUpsellConfig(null)}\r\n              className=\"absolute top-4 right-4 text-zinc-500 hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer\"\r\n            >\r\n              <X className=\"w-5 h-5\" />\r\n            </button>\r\n            <div className=\"w-16 h-16 bg-zinc-800 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-6 shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(254,154,0,0.2)]\">\r\n              <Lock className=\"w-8 h-8 text-[#fe9a00]\" />\r\n            </div>\r\n            <h2 className=\"text-2xl font-black italic uppercase tracking-tighter text-white mb-2\">\r\n              {upsellConfig.type === 'visitor' ? 'Account Required' : 'Premium Feature'}\r\n            </h2>\r\n            <p className=\"text-zinc-400 text-xs font-bold leading-relaxed mb-8\">{upsellConfig.message}</p>\r\n            <button\r\n              onClick={() => {\r\n                setUpsellConfig(null);\r\n                if (onLoginClick) onLoginClick();\r\n                else if (onNavigate) onNavigate({ action: 'login' });\r\n              }}\r\n              className=\"w-full bg-[#fe9a00] text-black font-black uppercase tracking-widest py-3 rounded hover:bg-white transition-colors cursor-pointer\"\r\n            >\r\n              Log In / Sign Up\r\n            </button>\r\n          </div>\r\n        </div>\r\n      )}\r\n\r\n      {/* Top Sticky Filter Header */}\r\n      <div className=\"sticky top-0 z-50 w-full       onClick={onBack}\r\n            className=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-zinc-400 hover:text-white transition-colors w-max cursor-pointer\"\r\n          >\r\n            <ArrowLeft 0. FETCH CURRENT MONTH ACTIVITY ACROSS ALL ENGAGEMENT TABLES FOR MONTHLY FAN RACE\r\n        const [\r\n          monthlyCompletedReadsRes,\r\n          monthlyReadingHistoryRes,\r\n          monthlyHypesRes,\r\n          monthlyReactsRes,\r\n          monthlyCommentsRes,\r\n          monthlySeriesLikesRes,\r\n          monthlyChapterLikesRes,\r\n          monthlyCharLikesRes,\r\n          monthlyCreatorLikesRes,\r\n          monthlyTipsRes,\r\n          monthlyTelemetryRes\r\n        ] = await Promise.all([\r\n          supabase\r\n            .from('completed_reads')\r\n            .select('user_id, chapter_id, created_at')\r\n            .gte('created_at', monthStartIso),\r\n          supabase\r\n            .from('reading_history')\r\n            .select('user_id, updated_at')\r\n            .gte('updated_at', monthStartIso),\r\n          supabase\r\n            .from('hypes')\r\n            .select('user_id, target_type, target_id, created_at')\r\n            .neq('target_type', 'comment')\r\n            .gte('created_at', monthStartIso),\r\n          supabase\r\n            .from('page_reacts')\r\n            .select('user_id, chapter_id, created_at')\r\n            .gte('created_at', monthStartIso),\r\n          supabase\r\n            .from('series_comments')\r\n            .select('user_id, series_slug, created_at')\r\n            .gte('created_at', monthStartIso),\r\n          supabase\r\n            .from('series_likes')\r\n            .select('user_id, series_slug, created_at')\r\n            .gte('created_at', monthStartIso),\r\n          supabase\r\n            .from('chapter_likes')\r\n            .select('user_id, chapter_id, created_at')\r\n            .gte('created_at', monthStartIso),\r\n          supabase\r\n            .from('character_likes')\r\n            .select('user_id, character_id, created_at')\r\n            .gte('created_at', monthStartIso),\r\n          supabase\r\n            .from('creator_likes')\r\n            .select('user_id, creator_id, created_at')\r\n            .gte('created_at', monthStartIso),\r\n          supabase\r\n            .from('creator_tips')\r\n            .select('user_id, amount_dollars, created_at')\r\n            .gte('created_at', monthStartIso),\r\n          supabase\r\n            .from('telemetry_events')\r\n            .select('user_id, created_at')\r\n            .not('user_id', 'is', null)\r\n            .gte('created_at', monthStartIso)\r\n            .limit(15000)\r\n        ]);\r\n\r\n        const monthlyFanScores = new Map<string, number>();\r\n        const activeLoginDaysByUser = new Map<string, Set<string>>();\r\n\r\n        const addMonthlyPoints = (uidRaw: any, pts: number) => {\r\n          const uid = String(uidRaw || '').trim();\r\n          if (!uid || excludedUserIds.has(uid)) return;\r\n          monthlyFanScores.set(uid, (monthlyFanScores.get(uid) || 0) + pts);\r\n        };\r\n\r\n        const registerUserLoginDay = (uidRaw: any, rawDate: any) => {\r\n          const uid = String(uidRaw || '').trim();\r\n          if (!uid || !rawDate || excludedUserIds.has(uid)) return;\r\n          const parsed = new Date(rawDate);\r\n          const dayStr = !isNaN(parsed.getTime())\r\n            ? getLocalCalendarDateKey(parsed)\r\n            : String(rawDate).slice(0, 10);\r\n          if (!dayStr) return;\r\n          if (!activeLoginDaysByUser.has(uid)) {\r\n            activeLoginDaysByUser.set(uid, new Set());\r\n          }\r\n          activeLoginDaysByUser.get(uid)!.add(dayStr);\r\n        };\r\n\r\n        // Direct Creator Tips (+10 pts min or 2x dollar amount)\r\n        (monthlyTipsRes.data || []).forEach((t: any) => {\r\n          const amt = Number(t.amount_dollars || 5);\r\n          addMonthlyPoints(t.user_id, Math.max(10, amt * 2));\r\n          registerUserLoginDay(t.user_id, t.created_at);\r\n        });\r\n\r\n        // Completed Chapter Reads (+10 pts per unique chapter read this month)\r\n        const seenMonthlyReads = new Set<string>();\r\n        (monthlyCompletedReadsRes.data || []).forEach((r: any) => {\r\n          const key = `${r.user_id}_${r.chapter_id}`;\r\n          if (!seenMonthlyReads.has(key)) {\r\n            seenMonthlyReads.add(key);\r\n            addMonthlyPoints(r.user_id, 10);\r\n          }\r\n          registerUserLoginDay(r.user_id, r.created_at);\r\n        });\r\n\r\n        // All Hypes (+10 pts each)\r\n        (monthlyHypesRes.data || []).forEach((h: any) => {\r\n          addMonthlyPoints(h.user_id, 10);\r\n          registerUserLoginDay(h.user_id, h.created_at);\r\n        });\r\n\r\n        // Series Discussion Comments (+3 pts)\r\n        (monthlyCommentsRes.data || []).forEach((c: any) => {\r\n          addMonthlyPoints(c.user_id, 3);\r\n          registerUserLoginDay(c.user_id, c.created_at);\r\n        });\r\n\r\n        // Smart Comments / Page Quick Reacts (+2 pts)\r\n        (monthlyReactsRes.data || []).forEach((pr: any) => {\r\n          addMonthlyPoints(pr.user_id, 2);\r\n          registerUserLoginDay(pr.user_id, pr.created_at);\r\n        });\r\n\r\n        // Likes (+1 pt each)\r\n        (monthlySeriesLikesRes.data || []).forEach((l: any) => {\r\n          addMonthlyPoints(l.user_id, 1);\r\n          registerUserLoginDay(l.user_id, l.created_at);\r\n        });\r\n        (monthlyChapterLikesRes.data || []).forEach((l: any) => {\r\n          addMonthlyPoints(l.user_id, 1);\r\n          registerUserLoginDay(l.user_id, l.created_at);\r\n        });\r\n        (monthlyCharLikesRes.data || []).forEach((l: any) => {\r\n          addMonthlyPoints(l.user_id, 1);\r\n          registerUserLoginDay(l.user_id, l.created_at);\r\n        });\r\n        (monthlyCreatorLikesRes.data || []).forEach((l: any) => {\r\n          addMonthlyPoints(l.user_id, 1);\r\n          registerUserLoginDay(l.user_id, l.created_at);\r\n        });\r\n\r\n        // Also count reading history & telemetry sessions for Daily Login Days (+5 pts per unique active day)\r\n        (monthlyReadingHistoryRes.data || []).forEach((rh: any) => {\r\n          registerUserLoginDay(rh.user_id, rh.updated_at);\r\n        });\r\n        (monthlyTelemetryRes.data || []).forEach((te: any) => {\r\n          registerUserLoginDay(te.user_id, te.created_at);\r\n        });\r\n\r\n        if (currentUser?.id && !excludedUserIds.has(String(currentUser.id))) {\r\n          registerUserLoginDay(currentUser.id, now.toISOString());\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        // Award +5 pts per unique daily login in the current month\r\n        activeLoginDaysByUser.forEach((daysSet, uid) => {\r\n          if (daysSet.size > 0) {\r\n            addMonthlyPoints(uid, daysSet.size * 5);\r\n          }\r\n        });\r\n\r\n        // Build deduplicated Top 10 Pure Fans list (prioritizing Monthly Points, backfilling up to 10 slots)\r\n        const seenUsernames = new Set<string>();\r\n        const seenIds = new Set<string>();\r\n        const allPureFanCandidates: any[] = [];\r\n\r\n        (allProfilesMeta || []).forEach((p: any) => {\r\n          const cid = String(p.id || '').trim();\r\n          if (!cid || excludedUserIds.has(cid) || isExcludedStaffOrTestAccount(p)) return;\r\n          if (!hasValidUniqueFanUsername(p)) return;\r\n\r\n          const monthlyPts = monthlyFanScores.get(cid) || 0;\r\n          const lifetimePts = Number(p.fandom_score || 0);\r\n          if (monthlyPts <= 0 && lifetimePts <= 0) return;\r\n\r\n          allPureFanCandidates.push({\r\n            id: cid,\r\n            username: String(p.username).trim(),\r\n            avatar_url: p.avatar_url || '',\r\n            frame_id: p.frame_id || p.avatar_frame_id || 'none',\r\n            is_premium: Boolean(p.is_premium),\r\n            score: monthlyPts,\r\n            lifetimeScore: lifetimePts\r\n          });\r\n        });\r\n\r\n        allPureFanCandidates.sort((a, b) => {\r\n          if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;\r\n          return b.lifetimeScore - a.lifetimeScore;\r\n        });\r\n\r\n        const finalFans: any[] = [];\r\n        for (const cand of allPureFanCandidates) {\r\n          const lowerName = cand.username.toLowerCase();\r\n          if (seenIds.has(cand.id) || seenUsernames.has(lowerName)) continue;\r\n          seenIds.add(cand.id);\r\n          seenUsernames.add(lowerName);\r\n          finalFans.push(cand);\r\n          if (finalFans.length >= 10) break;\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        const formattedTop10 = finalFans.slice(0, 10).map((fan: any, index: number) => {\r\n          let rankClass = 'C-Class Rank';\r\n          if (index < 3) rankClass = 'S-Class Rank';\r\n          else if (index < 6) rankClass = 'A-Class Rank';\r\n          else if (index < 9) rankClass = 'B-Class Rank';\r\n          return {\r\n            ...fan,\r\n            rank: index + 1,\r\n            class: rankClass,\r\n            isMostHypeTop5: index < 5\r\n          };\r\n        });\r\n\r\n        setSuperFans(formattedTop10);\r\n\r\n        // 2. PERSONAL USER RANK & AUTOMATIC MILESTONE NOTIFICATIONS (RANK 25, RANK 15, RANK 10)\r\n        if (currentUser?.id) {\r\n          const myProfile = profileById.get(String(currentUser.id));\r\n          const isCurrentUserExcluded =\r\n            isExcludedStaffOrTestAccount(currentUser) ||\r\n            (myProfile && isExcludedStaffOrTestAccount(myProfile)) ||\r\n            excludedUserIds.has(String(currentUser.id));\r\n\r\n          const myMonthlyScore = monthlyFanScores.get(String(currentUser.id)) || 0;\r\n          const myLifetimeScore = Number(\r\n            myProfile?.fandom_score ?? currentUser.fandom_score ?? 0\r\n          );\r\n          const isPlusUser = Boolean(currentUser?.is_premium || myProfile?.is_premium);\r\n\r\n          if (isCurrentUserExcluded) {\r\n            setUserRank({\r\n              rank: null,\r\n              isExcludedStaffOrCreator: true,\r\n              score: myMonthlyScore,\r\n              lifetimeScore: myLifetimeScore,\r\n              class: 'Official Creator / Staff'\r\n            });\r\n          } else {\r\n            const higherUniqueNames = new Set<string>();\r\n            allPureFanCandidates.forEach(c => {\r\n              if (c.id === String(currentUser.id)) return;\r\n              if (\r\n                c.score > myMonthlyScore ||\r\n                (c.score === myMonthlyScore && c.lifetimeScore > myLifetimeScore)\r\n              ) {\r\n                higherUniqueNames.add(c.username.toLowerCase());\r\n              }\r\n            });\r\n\r\n            const myRank = higherUniqueNames.size + 1;\r\n            let myClass = 'C-Class Rank';\r\n            if (myRank <= 3) myClass = 'S-Class Rank';\r\n            else if (myRank <= 6) myClass = 'A-Class Rank';\r\n            else if (myRank <= 9) myClass = 'B-Class Rank';\r\n\r\n            setUserRank({\r\n              rank: myRank,\r\n              score: myMonthlyScore,\r\n              lifetimeScore: myLifetimeScore,\r\n              class: myClass,\r\n              isExcludedStaffOrCreator: false\r\n            });\r\n\r\n            // Check if the user has reached a Rank Milestone (Rank 10, Rank 15, or Rank 25) this month\r\n            if (myMonthlyScore > 0 && myRank <= 25) {\r\n              let milestoneTier: 10 | 15 | 25 | null = null;\r\n              let title = '';\r\n              let headline = '';\r\n\r\n              if (myRank <= 10) {\r\n                milestoneTier = 10;\r\n                title = `Congrats! You Reached Rank #${myRank}!`;\r\n                headline =\r\n                  \"You're in the Top 10 and almost in the run to be one of the Top 5 Most Hype Fans of the Month!\";\r\n              } else if (myRank <= 15) {\r\n                milestoneTier = 15;\r\n                title = `Congrats! You Reached Rank #${myRank}!`;\r\n                headline =\r\n                  \"You're almost in the Top 10! Keep the momentum going to break into the top tier!\";\r\n              } else if (myRank <= 25) {\r\n                milestoneTier = 25;\r\n                title = `Congrats! You Reached Rank #${myRank}!`;\r\n                headline =\r\n                  \"You're almost in the Top 20! Keep hyping and reading to climb the monthly leaderboard!\";\r\n              }\r\n\r\n              if (milestoneTier) {\r\n                const seenMilestoneKey = `am_rank_milestone_${monthKey}_${currentUser.id}_tier_${milestoneTier}`;\r\n                if (!localStorage.getItem(seenMilestoneKey)) {\r\n                  localStorage.setItem(seenMilestoneKey, 'true');\r\n                  setMilestoneModal({\r\n                    milestoneTier,\r\n                    currentRank: myRank,\r\n                    title,\r\n                    headline,\r\n                    isPlusUser\r\n                  });\r\n                }\r\n              }\r\n            }\r\n          }\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        // 3. BIG 3 MONTHLY SERIES (Current Month Series Hypes +10 pts each + Total Hype Fallback)\r\n        const monthlyHypes = (monthlyHypesRes.data || []).filter(isFanActivity);\r\n\r\n        if (seriesList.length > 0) {\r\n          const hypeCounts: Record<string, number> = {};\r\n          monthlyHypes.forEach((h: any) => {\r\n            if (h.target_type === 'series' && h.target_id) {\r\n              hypeCounts[h.target_id] = (hypeCounts[h.target_id] || 0) + 10;\r\n            }\r\n          });\r\n\r\n          const rankedBig3 = seriesList\r\n            .filter((s: any) => !s.is_hidden)\r\n            .map((s: any) => ({\r\n              ...s,\r\n              hypeScore: (hypeCounts[s.slug] || 0) + (s.total_hype || s.hype_count || 0)\r\n            }))\r\n            .sort((a: any, b: any) => b.hypeScore - a.hypeScore);\r\n\r\n          setBig3(rankedBig3.slice(0, 3));\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        // 4. WEEKLY SERIES HYPES (Fan-Only Weekly Series Hypes +10 pts + Weekly Series Likes +1 pt)\r\n        const [weeklySeriesHypesRes, weeklySeriesLikesRes] = await Promise.all([\r\n          supabase\r\n            .from('hypes')\r\n            .select('target_id, created_at, user_id')\r\n            .eq('target_type', 'series')\r\n            .gte('created_at', lastSaturdayIso),\r\n          supabase\r\n            .from('series_likes')\r\n            .select('series_slug, created_at, user_id')\r\n            .gte('created_at', lastSaturdayIso)\r\n        ]);\r\n\r\n        if (seriesList.length > 0) {\r\n          const seriesWeeklyMap: Record<string, number> = {};\r\n\r\n          (weeklySeriesHypesRes.data || []).filter(isFanActivity).forEach((h: any) => {\r\n            const key = h.target_id;\r\n            if (key) seriesWeeklyMap[key] = (seriesWeeklyMap[key] || 0) + 10;\r\n          });\r\n\r\n          (weeklySeriesLikesRes.data || []).filter(isFanActivity).forEach((l: any) => {\r\n            if (l.series_slug) {\r\n              seriesWeeklyMap[l.series_slug] = (seriesWeeklyMap[l.series_slug] || 0) + 1;\r\n            }\r\n          });\r\n\r\n          const weeklyRankedSeries = [...seriesList]\r\n            .filter((s: any) => !s.is_hidden)\r\n            .map((s: any) => ({\r\n              ...s,\r\n              hypeScore: Math.max(seriesWeeklyMap[s.slug] || 0, s.weekly_hype || 0)\r\n            }))\r\n            .sort((a: any, b: any) => b.hypeScore - a.hypeScore);\r\n\r\n          setTopSeries(weeklyRankedSeries.slice(0, 5));\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        // 5. WEEKLY CREATOR HYPES (+10 pts) & Likes (+1 pt, Fan-Only)\r\n        const [allCreatorHypesRes, allCreatorLikesRes, allCreatorsDataRes] = await Promise.all([\r\n          supabase\r\n            .from('hypes')\r\n            .select('target_id, created_at, user_id')\r\n            .eq('target_type', 'creator')\r\n            .gte('created_at', lastSaturdayIso),\r\n          supabase\r\n            .from('creator_likes')\r\n            .select('creator_id, created_at, user_id')\r\n            .gte('created_at', lastSaturdayIso),\r\n          supabase\r\n            .from('series_creators')\r\n            .select('name, avatar_url, role, series_slug')\r\n            .eq('is_visible', true)\r\n        ]);\r\n\r\n        const allCreatorsData = allCreatorsDataRes.data;\r\n        if (allCreatorsData) {\r\n          const creatorHypeCounts: Record<string, number> = {};\r\n          (allCreatorHypesRes.data || []).filter(isFanActivity).forEach((h: any) => {\r\n            creatorHypeCounts[h.target_id] = (creatorHypeCounts[h.target_id] || 0) + 10;\r\n          });\r\n          (allCreatorLikesRes.data || []).filter(isFanActivity).forEach((l: any) => {\r\n            creatorHypeCounts[l.creator_id] = (creatorHypeCounts[l.creator_id] || 0) + 1;\r\n          });\r\n\r\n          const uniqueCreatorsMap = new Map();\r\n          allCreatorsData.forEach((c: any) => {\r\n            if (!uniqueCreatorsMap.has(c.name)) uniqueCreatorsMap.set(c.name, c);\r\n          });\r\n\r\n          const rankedCreators = Array.from(uniqueCreatorsMap.values())\r\n            .map((c: any) => ({ ...c, hypeScore: creatorHypeCounts[c.name] || 0 }))\r\n            .sort((a, b) => b.hypeScore - a.hypeScore)\r\n            .slice(0, 5);\r\n\r\n          setTopCreators(rankedCreators);\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        // 6. WEEKLY CHARACTER HYPES (+10 pts) & Likes (+1 pt, Fan-Only)\r\n        const [allCharHypesRes, allCharLikesRes, allCharsDataRes] = await Promise.all([\r\n          supabase\r\n            .from('hypes')\r\n            .select('target_id, created_at, user_id')\r\n            .eq('target_type', 'character')\r\n            .gte('created_at', lastSaturdayIso),\r\n          supabase\r\n            .from('character_likes')\r\n            .select('character_id, created_at, user_id')\r\n            .gte('created_at', lastSaturdayIso),\r\n          supabase\r\n            .from('series_characters')\r\n            .select('id, name, headshot_url, series_slug')\r\n            .eq('is_hidden', false)\r\n        ]);\r\n\r\n        const allCharsData = allCharsDataRes.data;\r\n        if (allCharsData) {\r\n          const charHypeCounts: Record<string, number> = {};\r\n          (allCharHypesRes.data || []).filter(isFanActivity).forEach((h: any) => {\r\n            charHypeCounts[String(h.target_id)] =\r\n              (charHypeCounts[String(h.target_id)] || 0) + 10;\r\n          });\r\n          (allCharLikesRes.data || []).filter(isFanActivity).forEach((l: any) => {\r\n            charHypeCounts[String(l.character_id)] =\r\n              (charHypeCounts[String(l.character_id)] || 0) + 1;\r\n          });\r\n\r\n          const rankedChars = allCharsData\r\n            .map((c: any) => {\r\n              const sTitle =\r\n                seriesList.find((s: any) => s.slug === c.series_slug)?.title ||\r\n                'Unknown Series';\r\n              return {\r\n                ...c,\r\n                series_title: sTitle,\r\n                hypeScore: charHypeCounts[String(c.id)] || 0\r\n              };\r\n            })\r\n            .sort((a, b) => b.hypeScore - a.hypeScore)\r\n            .slice(0, 5);\r\n\r\n          setTopCharacters(rankedChars);\r\n        }\r\n\r\n        // 7. WEEKLY TOP CHAPTERS\r\n        const [chapterHypesRes, chapterLikesRes, chapterReactsRes, chapterReadsRes] =\r\n          await Promise.all([\r\n            supabase\r\n              .from('hypes')\r\n              .select('target_id, user_id')\r\n              .eq('target_type', 'chapter')\r\n              .gte('created_at', lastSaturdayIso),\r\n            supabase\r\n any) => {\r\n          if (rd.chapter_id)\r\n            chapterInteractionCounts[rd.chapter_id] =\r\n              (chapterInteractionCounts[rd.chapter_id] || 0) + 1;\r\n        });\r\n\r\n        const trendingChapIds = Object.keys(chapterInteractionCounts);\r\n\r\n        if (trendingChapIds.length > 0) {\r\n          const { data: chapsData } = await supabase\r\n            .from('chapters')\r\n            .select('id, title, chapter_number, series_slug, thumbnail_url')\r\n            .in('id', trendingChapIds);\r\n\r\n          if (chapsData) {\r\n            const rankedChaps = chapsData\r\n              .map((c: any) => {\r\n                const sData = seriesList.find((s: any) => s.slug === c.series_slug);\r\n                return {\r\n                  ...c,\r\n                  series_title: sData?.title || 'Unknown Series',\r\n                  cover_url: sData?.cover_url,\r\n                  hypeScore: chapterInteractionCounts[c.id] || 0\r\n                };\r\n              })\r\n              .sort((a: any, b: any) => b.hypeScore - a.hypeScore);\r\n\r\n            setTopWeeklyChapters(rankedChaps.slice(0, 3));\r\n          }\r\n        } '&' : '?';\r\n    clean = `${clean}${sep}am_retry=1&t=${Date.now()}`;\r\n  } else if (attempt >= 3) {\r\n    // Attempt 3: Normalized URI + fresh cache-buster\r\n    try {\r\n      const base = clean.split('?')[0];\r\n      const decoded = decodeURIComponent(base);\r\n      const encoded = encodeURI(decoded).replace(/#/g, '%23');\r\n      clean = `${encoded}?am_retry=${attempt}&t=${Date.now()}`;\r\n    } catch {\r\n      const sep = clean.includes('?') ? setFailedPermanently(false);\r\n    setIsRetrying(false);\r\n  }, [src]);\r\n\r\n  const resolvedSrc = normalizeMangaPageUrl(src, attempt);\r\n\r\n  const handleError = () => {\r\n    if (attempt < 3) );\r\n});\r\n\r\nconst renderContentWithLinks = (text: string) => {\r\n  if (!text) return null;\r\n  const splitRegex = /((?:https?:\\/\\/|www\\.)[^\\s]+)/g;\r\n  const matchRegex = };\r\n    fetchAds();\r\n  }, []);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (!chapterId) return;\r\n    const fetchHotspots = async () => {\r\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\r\n   };\r\n  }, [chapterId]);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    let viewportMeta = document.querySelector('meta[name=\"viewport\"]');\r\n    let originalContent = '';\r\n    if (viewportMeta) {\r\n }\r\n\r\n      if (!activeUserId) {\r\n        setCurrentPage(0);\r\n        setIsLoadingProgress(false);\r\n        return;\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      try {\r\n        const { data } = await fetchProgress();\r\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\r\n  }, [chapterId, userId, isAuthLoaded, initialPage, pages.length]);\r\n\r\n  const saveProgressToDB =       console.error('Failed to save progress:', error);\r\n      }\r\n    },\r\n    [chapterId]\r\n  );\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => {\r\n    if (isLoadingProgress) return;\r\n    const saveTimer = );\r\n\r\n  const handleNextChapter = useCallback(\r\n    (e?: any) => {\r\n      if (e) {\r\n        e.preventDefault?.();\r\n        e.stopPropagation?.();\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      const activeUserId } else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {\r\n        if (mode === 'horizontal') {\r\n          e.preventDefault();\r\n          if (isRTL) {\r\n            if (!showEndPromptRef.current) goNext();\r\n currentScaleRef.current > 1.05 ||\r\n        isAnimatingPage\r\n      )\r\n        return;\r\n      touchCurrentPos.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };\r\n      const deltaX = e.clientX - setIsDragging(false);\r\n        return;\r\n      }\r\n      if (\r\n        document\r\n          .elementsFromPoint(e.clientX, e.clientY)\r\n          .some(\r\n            el =>\r\n              el.classList.contains('hotspot-button') || el.classList.contains('reader-nav-arrow')\r\n          )\r\n      ) {\r\n        setIsDragging(false);\r\n        return;\r\n      }\r\n\r\n      if {\r\n          if (isRTL) goNext();\r\n          else goPrev();\r\n        } else if (x > width * 0.62) {\r\n          if (isRTL) goPrev();\r\n          else goNext();\r\n        } else {\r\n setIsAnimatingPage(true);\r\n        const direction = deltaX < 0 ? -1 : 1;\r\n        setDragOffset(direction * window.innerWidth);\r\n        setTimeout(() => {\r\n          if (isRTL) {\r\n Number(ch.chapter_number) < Number(existing.chapter.chapter_number)\r\n          : Number(ch.chapter_number) > Number(existing.chapter.chapter_number);\r\n\r\n        highestChapterBySeries.set(slug, {\r\n          chapter: shouldReplaceChapter ? ch : existing.chapter,\r\n          latestSeriesActivityMs: bestActivityMs,\r\n          isActiveOsw: existing.isActiveOsw || isActiveOsw\r\n        });\r\n      }\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    // If an active One-Shot Wednesday series has no chapter row loaded in `latestChapters` yet, synthesize a direct card so it still appears in Latest Chapters while unlocked\r\n    seriesList.forEach((s: any) => {\r\n      if (!s?.slug || s.is_hidden) return;\r\n      const slug = String(s.slug);\r\n      if (highestChapterBySeries.has(slug)) return;\r\n      const isActiveOsw =\r\n        isActiveOneShotWednesday(s, seriesList) &&\r\n        getOneShotRemainingMs(s, seriesList) > 0;\r\n      if (isActiveOsw) {\r\n        const sTime = new Date(s.publish_at || s.created_at || 0).getTime();\r\n        highestChapterBySeries.set(slug, {\r\n          chapter: {\r\n            id: `osw_${slug}`,\r\n            series_slug: slug,\r\n            chapter_number: 1,\r\n            title: s.title || 'One-Shot Wednesday',\r\n            thumbnail_url: s.cover_url || s.character_url || '',\r\n            is_published: true,\r\n            publish_at: s.publish_at || s.created_at\r\n          },\r\n          latestSeriesActivityMs: isNaN(sTime) ? 0 : sTime,\r\n          isActiveOsw: true\r\n        });\r\n      }\r\n    });\r\n\r\n    const allSeriesEntries = Array.from(highestChapterBySeries.values());\r\n\r\n    // Keep active One-Shot Wednesday unlocked stories in `freshEntries` ONLY while their unlock timer is > 0.\r\n    // Once the unlock window ends, `entry.isActiveOsw` becomes false and older Completed Runs automatically leave Latest Chapters!\r\n    const freshEntries = allSeriesEntries.filter(\r\n      entry => entry.isActiveOsw || now - entry.latestSeriesActivityMs <= maxLatestAgeMs\r\n    );\r\n    const candidatePool = freshEntries.length >= 6 ? Always spotlight active One-Shot Wednesday unlocked stories at the front of Latest Chapters while unlocked\r\n        if (a.isActiveOsw && !b.isActiveOsw) return -1;\r\n        if (!a.isActiveOsw && 1;\r\n\r\n        return b.latestSeriesActivityMs - a.latestSeriesActivityMs;\r\n      })\r\n      .map(entry => ({\r\n        ...entry.chapter,\r\n        isActiveOsw: entry.isActiveOsw,\r\n        isAlreadyRead: readChapterIdSet.has(String(entry.chapter.id)),\r\n        publish_at:\r\n          entry.latestSeriesActivityMs > 0\r\n            ? new [];\r\n        data.forEach((row: any) => {\r\n          const cid = String(row.chapter_id);\r\n          const createdMs = row.created_at ? new Date(row.created_at).getTime() : Number(localMap[cid] mergedTimes.sort((a, b) => a - b);\r\n        setDailyUnlocksUsed(mergedTimes.length);\r\n        setOldestDailyUnlockMs(mergedTimes.length > 0 ? mergedTimes[0] : null);\r\n        setUnlockedChapters(Array.from(validTwelveHourChapterIds));\r\n        return;\r\n      }\r\n    } catch (err) { console.error('Error fetching temporary unlocks:', err); }\r\n\r\n    recent24hTimestamps.sort((a, b) => a - b);\r\n    setDailyUnlocksUsed(recent24hTimestamps.length);\r\n    setOldestDailyUnlockMs(recent24hTimestamps.length > 0 ? recent24hTimestamps[0] : null);\r\n    setUnlockedChapters(Array.from(validTwelveHourChapterIds));\r\n  }, [userTier, currentUserId]);\r\n\r\n  useEffect(() => { syncTemporaryUnlocks(); }, [syncTemporaryUnlocks, nowTick]);\r\n\r\n  const 'Oldest' : 'Newest'}\r\n                  </button>\r\n                </div>\r\n              )}\r\n\r\n              {displayedChapters.map((ch: any) => {\r\n                const absoluteIndex = chapters.findIndex(c => c.id === ch.id);\r\n                const actualProgress = readProgresses[String(ch.id)] || 0;\r\n                const hasTempUnlock = !isCompletedRun && rewardedAdsEnabled && unlockedChapters.includes(String(ch.id));\r\n                const isLocked = checkIsLocked(ch.id) && !hasTempUnlock;\r\n                const isLatestChapterOfSeries = absoluteIndex === chapters.length - 1;\r\n                const showNewBadge = isLatestChapterOfSeries && isNewRelease(ch.publish_at, ch.created_at);\r\n                const displayReacts = (ch.react_count || 0) + (chapterStats[String(ch.id)]?.reacts || 0);\r\n\r\n                return (\r\n                  <div key={ch.id} onClick={() => handleReadChapter(ch, absoluteIndex)} className=\"flex items-start sm:items-center gap-3 sm:gap-6 mb-4 hover:bg-zinc-900/80 p-3 sm:p-4 rounded-xl transition-all cursor-pointer border border-transparent hover:border-zinc-800 group\">\r\n                    <div className=\"relative overflow-hidden rounded-lg min-w-[80px] w-20 h-20 sm:min-w-[128px] sm:w-32 sm:h-32 flex-shrink-0\">\r\n                      <img src={ch.thumbnail_url || `${CLOUDFLARE_BASE_URL}/assets/placeholder-thumb.jpg`} loading=\"lazy\" className={`w-full h-full object-cover bg-zinc-800 transition-transform duration-500 ${isLocked ? 'opacity-40 grayscale group-hover:scale-105' : 'group-hover:scale-110'}`} style={{ objectPosition: ch.thumbnail_position || '50% 50%', transform: `scale(${(ch.thumbnail_scale || 100) / 100})` }} alt=\"Thumbnail\" />\r\n                      <div className=\"absolute inset-0 bg-black/20 group-hover:bg-transparent transition-colors\" />\r\n                      {isLocked && (\r\n                        <div className=\"absolute top-1 left-1 sm:top-2 sm:left-2 bg-black/80 border border-zinc-700 p-1 sm:p-1.5 rounded-md\">\r\n                          <Lock className=\"w-2.5 h-2.5 sm:w-3 sm:h-3 text-zinc-400\" />\r\n                        </div>\r\n                      )}\r\n                      {freeWindowCountdownText ? (\r\n                        <div className=\"absolute top-1 right-1 z-20 bg-[#fe9a00] text-black text-[7px] sm:text-[8px] font-black uppercase tracking-tight px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(254,154,0,0.8)] pointer-events-none\">⏳ {freeWindowCountdownText}</div>\r\n                      ) : showNewBadge && (\r\n                        <div className=\"absolute top-1 right-1 z-20 bg-red-600 text-white text-[7px] sm:text-[8px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(220,38,38,0.8)] animate-pulse pointer-events-none\">NEW!</div>\r\n                      )}\r\n                    </div>\r\n\r\n                    <div className=\"flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center flex-1 min-w-0 gap-3 sm:gap-4 w-full\">\r\n                      <div className=\"flex-1 min-w-0 w-full\">\r\n                        <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 sm:gap-3 mb-1\">\r\n                          <p className={`text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-black tracking-widest uppercase ${isLocked ?                         </div>\r\n                              <span className=\"text-[8px] sm:text-[10px] font-black text-zinc-500\">{isLocked ? 0 : actualProgress}%</span>\r\n                            </div>\r\n                          </div>\r\n                        )}\r\n                      </div>\r\n\r\n                      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between      </button>\r\n                  <div className=\"w-full max-w-xs mx-auto mb-8 [&_button]:w-full [&_button]:justify-center\">\r\n                    <HypeButton targetType=\"creator\" </div>\r\n                  <div className=\"flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-3 w-full\">\r\n                    {c.twitter_url && <a href={c.twitter_url} target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer\"         <button onClick={() => window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' })} className=\"flex flex-col items-center gap-2 group cursor-pointer\">\r\n              <div className=\"p-3 bg-zinc-900   }
